# Activity Vault — WAVELOG

Running record of build waves. Newest first.

## Rotation 27 photo-truth fix — 2026-10-14 — VP-0024 / VP-0025 / VP-0026 / VP-0027 closed

The fleet photo-truth audit read every placed photo from the image itself and found 50 mismatches in the Vault (keyword/pun sourcing: photos matched to an activity's words, not the activity), plus a designed-absence leak, a byte-identical duplicate pair, and one unreadable image. Fix: **18 placements replaced** with Wikimedia Commons photographs that depict the activity itself (children painting pumpkins, eggs in dye cups, children carrying lanterns in a night parade, hands folding a dumpling, children making paper at a vat, and 13 more; provenance per photo in the data record), and **36 placements pulled to designed absence**, files deleted. The leak (Story Building Blocks' `wooden-blocks.webp` cover) is off and the r26 designed-absence closure stands; Pumpkin Bowling keeps a new distinct USDA lane photo, Mini Pumpkin Bowling's duplicate is pulled, so no file serves two activities anywhere (full-tree hash scan: zero duplicate groups). Counts, honestly restated: data-carried photos **273 → 240**, rendered covers **277 → 241**; the test-r23 floor was re-derived down to 240 in the same commit (floors state the truth, never protect a number). The 368 designed-absence records' data is untouched and pulled activities do not join the remainder list. Full per-placement record: `~/workspace/fleet-ops/wave-engine/landing-activity-r27-phototruth.md`.

## Rotation 20 follow-through — 2026-10-13 — filters.json age declaration corrected to the data's true span

Research cycle 139 named the defect: `data/v1/filters.json` declared the age filter's span as 5–12 while the database it filters serves ages 3–14 — the declaration silently excluded the youngest cohort and the entire Just Teens 13–14 band (120 activities per age at wave 43) from the filter's stated range. Disposition (wave engine, this rotation): FIX, at the generator source. Bounds recomputed from all 747 activities in `data/v1/activities.json` (age_min spans 3–13, age_max spans 3–14), then `scripts/build.py`'s filters block and the generated `data/v1/filters.json` corrected in the same commit (min 5→3, max 12→14) so the next build reproduces the corrected declaration. No activity data changed; T-ACT-1 remains deferred by name; T-ACT-6…16 untouched by this fix and still open.

## Wave 43 — 2026-10-13 — Rotation 20: "The Minimal Pass + Just Teens, a Fifth Time — the Floor Rises to Nine, and the Vault Becomes a Database" 🗄️

**The headlines, named before building (the user's rotation-20 order, two parts): (1) THE MINIMAL-DESIGN PASS** — the Vault's turn in the fleet sequence: teacher's-desk identity expressed at the fleet's sleekest professional register, scoped by rotation 19 as global header/nav first, then homepage, collections, activity pages, tool pages — presentation only, everything kept. **(2) WAVE 43, the living database grows** — the site's stated ambition is now "a live database of all activities ever," kept current by The Scout, presented AS a database; and the standing growth wave, sized from this wave's fresh audit exactly as waves 39–42 sized theirs. The audit recomputed per-age coverage at wave 42's close — age 3: **160**, age 4: **160**, age 5: 329, age 6: 426, age 7: 496, age 8: 530, age 9: 524, age 10: 518, age 11: 456, age 12: 446, age 13: **112**, age 14: **112** — and inside the 13/14 band the primary cells measured seasons **8**, outdoor **8**, pe-movement **8**, music-rhythm **8**, rainy-day **8**, icebreakers 9, crafts 9, stem 10, holidays 10, quiet-focus 15, team-community 19 — a floor of **8** shared by five categories. The eight thinnest cells are the five at 8, the two at 9, and one of the two tied at 10; the tie breaks on the tag shelves, honestly and in the open (the wave-41 method): the holidays record carries the St. Patrick's Day tag, standing at **6** overall — one of the six floor holiday tags — while no STEM tag shelf is anywhere near as thin. **The headlines to land:** minimal pass on every surface named below; age-13/14 coverage 112 → **120**, floor **8 → 9**, total 739 → **747**, collections 31 → **32** at the honest 8-pick minimum, benchmark gap 48 → **40** per age.

**Part 1 — The minimal pass, per surface (before → after, named fixes):**
- **Global header/nav (the busiest element sitewide — FIXED FIRST, generator-owned):** before, every page carried 8 manila folder-tab primary links wrapping across three rows plus a 9-link secondary row scattered beside them, under a punched binder-hole strip (see the rotation-020 before capture: brand low-left, tabs stacked, seasons floating right). After: **one primary row** — Browse, The Desk, Plan my week, Collections, Kit Bag, Party Pack (the VP-0002 pair stays in primary) — plus **one "More" disclosure** grouping the five remaining tools (Day Planner, Kit, Sub Tub, Party Planner, Field Trips) and all five season hubs; binder-hole strip removed, hairline rule, plain professional link type, calm toggle kept. All 17 destinations survive on all **766 pages**; the rewrite is generator-owned (`inject_minimal_headers()` in `scripts/build.py`, applied after placement every build, aria-current preserved per page) and locked per page in `test-wave43.js` (nav-more present, nav-secondary absent, exactly one primary nav).
- **Homepage (progressive disclosure below the hero):** before, the hero was followed by a wall of eight promo bands (five seasonal + three flagship) before the desk. After: the hero itself carries the database framing (below), a published **database-coverage strip** sits under the stats (per-age coverage, the thinnest shelf named with its 40-gap, the Scout queue linked), and all eight promo bands live behind **one disclosure** — "Seasonal highlights & flagship tools" — content entire, nothing deleted. The desk drawers and the browse grid are unchanged below it.
- **Collections (detail panels on-demand):** before, browse mode rendered all 31 collection detail sections as one long wall under the grid. After: browse mode shows the grid alone; a detail panel opens **only when routed to** (card click or deep link) — `collections.js` `applyRoute` now hides every section unless it is the routed one. The Fifth Time capture shows the panel entire, all 8 picks with curator notes.
- **Activity pages (rating/reflection/related behind disclosure):** the rating form + reflection now sit inside a `rate-disclosure` ("Rate this activity, add a note, or write a reflection") and the related-activities list inside a `related-disclosure`, on all 747 pages via the generator template; the binder furniture is quieter (holes/rings at 45% opacity, filed stamp at 3°). All rating/related function is untouched — the widgets work identically inside the disclosures.
- **Tool pages (one primary action per view):** all eight tool pages (Planner, Day, Kit, Kit Bag, Party Pack, Sub Tub, Party, Field Trip) now carry exactly **one solid primary action** (their build/compose button, marked `primary-action` and asserted singular per page in the new suite); every other button on those pages recedes to ghost under the `tool-page` register. Forms, results and print flows unchanged.
- **Browse grid (named fix):** the alternating ±0.45° card rotation and oversized washi strips read busy/dated next to the fleet bar — cards are now flat and aligned, washi reduced to a quiet 84px strip at 65% opacity; the index-card identity (margin line, ruled lines, stamp chips) is kept. Filters panel shadow flattened.
- **Kept, deliberately:** the desk and its eleven drawers, the stamp brand, the sticky curator note, the squiggle, all seasonal content, all 747 records and 32 collections and every tool function — the pass removed chrome and sprawl, never content (the user's standing rule for minimal passes, and the same moderation the fleet applied to Aster this rotation).

**Part 2 — Live-database framing:** the homepage title is now "Activity Vault — the live activity database for teachers"; the hero kicker reads "The live activity database · kept current by The Scout"; the lede states what a record is (complete: setup, materials, steps, variations, safety, inclusion), that the database grows every wave, and that **its gaps are published, not hidden** — and the coverage strip publishes them, with the Scout's human-decided review queue linked as the database's intake. Search/filter/browse was already database-grade (faceted filters, deep links, per-record pages, a public API + llms.txt); the framing now honestly names and evidences it on the front door.

**Part 3 — Eight activities banded for thirteen- and fourteen-year-olds only (+8 → 747), one per the band's eight thinnest cells:** `growing-degree-day-ledger` (seasons 8 → 9 — a season's heat counted daily into growing degree-days, a budburst prediction entered in ink and graded by the plant itself), `microclimate-map-survey` (outdoor 8 → 9 — temperature, shade and wind read at a grid of stations inside one ten-minute window and drawn as a map of one yard's many climates), `kin-ball-first-session` (pe-movement 8 → 9 — three teams, one enormous ball, no owner: serve by calling a colour, win by being hardest to catch out), `conducting-patterns-studio` (music-rhythm 8 → 9 — beat patterns in two, three and four, cueing, clean cut-offs, a podium run as a rota and judged by a log), `one-table-micro-museum` (rainy-day 8 → 9 — a real museum on one table: objects on loan under law, labels checked by their owners, everything signed home entire), `sashiko-stitch-sampler-studio` (crafts 9 → 10 — counted running stitch in indigo and white, grids drawn before they are sewn, wandering rows unpicked by their own makers), `personal-map-introductions` (icebreakers 9 → 10 — a drawn map of the places that made you, toured in three stations, blank regions unasked and entire), `celtic-knot-drawing-studio` (holidays 10 → 11 — one unbroken line woven over-under on a dot grid for St. Patrick's Day, traced strand by strand before ink is allowed). **The headline, landed:** age-13 coverage 112 → **120**, age-14 coverage 112 → **120**, every other age unchanged (3 and 4 still 160 each; 12 still 446); the 13/14 primary floor rises **8 → 9**; 13–14-only records 36 → **44**. Every primary total rises only by its aimed delta: seasons 48 → 49, outdoor 63 → 64, pe-movement 74 → 75, music-rhythm 43 → 44, rainy-day 42 → 43, crafts 101 → 102, icebreakers 50 → 51, holidays 55 → 56; all other primaries unchanged (stem 122, team-community 78, quiet-focus 63). Wave 42's own file re-verified at 5,397 assertions, wave 39's at 5,469, partypack at 26,588, dayplanner at 13,513, kitbag at 1,036.

**Honest assessment:** this rotation did two kinds of work and kept them from contaminating each other. The minimal pass changed how the Vault *presents* — one nav row where seventeen links sprawled across three, a promo wall behind one disclosure, collection panels that open when asked, ratings behind a summary line, one loud button per tool — and removed not a single record, collection, link destination or function to do it; the before/after captures show the same desk, swept. The growth wave then ran the teen campaign the way it has run since wave 39: measure first, size the pack from the cells, break the tie in the open, cut the near-duplicates before they run, and let the collection land where the validator's rule honestly sits — at its exact minimum, unpadded, a fifth time. And the framing caught up with the fact: the site now calls itself a live database on its front door and publishes its own coverage — including the shelf that is still short — where a visitor can check it. What this rotation refused is on the record too: T-ACT-1 deferred by name rather than half-built, no coding record bolted onto the tied STEM cell, no ninth record to reach a tidier shelf, and no claim that the 40-wide remaining gap to the young end moved more than its honest eight. The next honest debts, in the audit's own order: the 13/14 band's total is still the thinnest (120 against 160 — a sixth Just Teens wave, aimed at stem, crafts, icebreakers and the new floor of 9, would be a real wave), T-ACT-1 (printables) remains the filed lead candidate awaiting its rotation, and the drama/coding lane remains the named gap awaiting a wave briefed to build it properly. The working-vs-live gap (747 working vs the 455-activity live snapshot) remains parked for the user's explicit push order, untouched as instructed.

## Wave 42 — 2026-10-13 — "Just Teens, a Fourth Time — the Floor Rises to Eight" 🌕

**The headline, named before building (from this wave's fresh data audit, measured first, nothing assumed):** the audit recomputed per-age primary coverage across the whole archive at wave 41's close — age 3: **160**, age 4: **160**, age 5: 329, age 6: 426, age 7: 496, age 8: 530, age 9: 524, age 10: 518, age 11: 456, age 12: 446, age 13: **104**, age 14: **104** — and the thinnest band is still ages 13 and 14, at sixty-five hundredths of the young-end parity figure. Inside that band the primary-category cells measure: seasons **7**, outdoor **7**, pe-movement **7**, music-rhythm **7**, rainy-day **7**, icebreakers 8, crafts 8, stem 9, holidays 10, quiet-focus 15, team-community 19 — a floor of **7**, shared by five categories. The pack is sized from those cells the same way waves 39, 40 and 41 sized theirs: the eight thinnest cells in the band are the five at the floor of 7, the two at 8, and stem at 9 — **one record for each of those eight cells is eight records, no more, no fewer**. **The headline to land:** age-13 coverage 104 → **112**, age-14 coverage 104 → **112**, every other age unchanged; the 13/14 primary floor rises **7 → 8** (every category in the band now carries at least 8 primaries); total 731 → **739**; and eight honest picks sit inside the build validator's 8–12 rule at exactly its minimum, so a 31st collection unlocks — "Just Teens, a Fourth Time."

**Part 1 — Eight activities banded for thirteen- and fourteen-year-olds only (+8 → 739), one per the band's eight thinnest cells:** `overwintering-garlic-planting-crew` (seasons 7 → 8 — garlic planted in autumn for a summer harvest: cloves set pointed-up at a measured depth, the bed tenancy agreed in writing across the whole cycle, the winter logged honestly including the months nobody visited, spring emergence counted shoot by shoot and claimed only where it was seen), `dusk-bat-exit-count` (outdoor 7 → 8 — a known roost counted out at dusk from one set watch point: sectors, silence, tallies by the minute, lost minutes marked instead of invented, the roost's location never disclosed, and no bat ever approached, lit or handled), `tchoukball-first-session` (pe-movement 7 → 8 — a no-contact team game where shots rebound off a frame and the catch is the defence; the forbidden zone stays empty while a shot is live, landings are judged once, settled and chalked), `song-arranging-studio-first-session` (music-rhythm 7 → 8 — one permitted song taken apart to its skeleton and rebuilt: one change at a time, predictions entered in the log before they are heard, kept and cut verdicts in the same ink, the whole room credited as arrangers), `one-room-documentary-crew` (rainy-day 7 → 8 — a short documentary about one real room, consent before camera every time, declines honoured in the plan, interviews strikable after the fact, nothing posted or uploaded, and the room's people seeing the cut before any other audience), `spectrum-line-introductions` (icebreakers 8 → 9 — a screened, low-stakes deck answered with feet first along a taped line, then two minutes of talk with the person furthest from your spot; PASS and NOT SURE are full positions, nobody's place is recorded or photographed), `pamphlet-stitch-bookbinding-studio` (crafts 8 → 9 — a real blank book bound by hand: grain with the spine, holes measured before they are punched at the adult's sharp station alone, a stitched spine inspected by a friend, a cover cut to the book in hand), `solar-oven-temperature-trials` (stem 9 → 10 — box ovens trialled against a shared control, one variable at a time, held-constant lists countersigned by a rival team, temperatures logged by the minute, and a cloud-ruined trial declared void in the log without flinching). **The headline, landed:** age-13 coverage 104 → **112**, age-14 coverage 104 → **112**, every other age unchanged (3 and 4 still 160 each — wave 38's parity untouched; 12 still 446); the age-13/14 primary floor rises **7 → 8**; 13–14-only records 28 → **36**. Every primary total rises only by its aimed delta: seasons 47 → 48, outdoor 62 → 63, pe-movement 73 → 74, music-rhythm 42 → 43, rainy-day 41 → 42, icebreakers 49 → 50, crafts 100 → 101, stem 121 → 122; all other primaries unchanged (holidays 55, team-community 78, quiet-focus 63). Enrichment: copy_free 351 → 359, no_elimination 153 → 161, inclusion 213 → 221 (all eight carry all three). Tag deltas aimed at thin shelves: summer 35 → 37 (the thinnest season tag rises twice — the bats' active season and the solar trials' sun), fall 54 → 55 (the garlic planting), winter 119 → 120 (the garlic's overwintering). Near-dupe screen in `scripts/add_wave42.py` — worst token Jaccard 0.23 (`one-room-documentary-crew` vs `mlk-day-service-sprint`); every record is long-form class (1,852–2,170 words of record, asserted ≥ 1,300 in the suite). **Two drafted records were cut before the pack ran, on the standing discipline:** a guided constellation tour for the outdoor slot — `star-party-hosts` (wave 40) already hosts a night-sky tour rehearsed from that night's chart, and older guides do not make it a new record — and a speed-introductions carousel for the icebreakers slot — `two-minute-mingle` already runs many short chats against a clock, and faster chairs do not make it a new record. The eight that resulted are not filler around a number: a compost heap with a season-long temperature ledger, a forecast desk graded by its own mornings, preserving under a tested recipe's authority, a trail crew working under permission, a star party hosted in protected darkness, a stream graded by its own citizens, a circuit designed by its coaches, and games built to survive strangers. ## Wave 39 — 2026-10-13 — "Just Teens, a First Time — the Next Age Band's Turn" 🧭

**The headline, named before building (from this wave's data audit):** parity closed the young end (160 against 160), so the audit recomputed per-age and per-primary-category coverage across the whole archive and found the thinnest shelf at the OTHER end of childhood: ages 13 and 14 carried **76** activities each against **446** at age 12 — a cliff, not a slope — and inside that band the primary-category cells were the thinnest in the vault: seasons **1**, outdoor **2**, pe-movement **3**, music-rhythm **5**, rainy-day **5**, icebreakers **6**, crafts **6**, holidays **8**, stem **8**, quiet-focus 14, team-community 18. Almost everything up there is spillover from records written for younger rooms (bands like 5–14 and 8–14); almost nothing was ever written FOR thirteen- and fourteen-year-olds. So this wave mirrors the Little Hands shape at the top end: **twelve records banded 13–14 only** — a band this wave invents (no record previously started at 13) — one for every primary category plus a second for seasons, the floor.

**Part 1 — Twelve activities banded for thirteen- and fourteen-year-olds only (+12 → 715):** `adopt-a-tree-year-log` and `seed-library-opening-day` (seasons 1 → 3 — a tree adopted for a whole year, measured monthly at a loose ribbon, its log handed to next year's custodians with the boring months recorded as honestly as the dramatic ones; and a seed lending library whose packets carry the germination percentage its own test bench actually grew, repaid in seed from borrowers' strongest plants), `compass-course-three-flags` (outdoor 2 → 3 — real bearings and calibrated pace counts; no compass record existed anywhere on the shelf), `referee-run-round-robin` (pe-movement 3 → 4 — teens officiate their own tournament: fixture card with no referee ever judging their own team, scoresheets audited in public, a dispute protocol whose whole shape is call, one sentence, play resumes), `ten-minute-set-handover` (music-rhythm 5 → 6 — teen DJs hand a live floor to the next DJ on a planned bridge track, under a volume cap marked on the equipment and a lyrics rule screened in advance, track by track, entire), `indoor-clue-chain-architects` (rainy-day 5 → 6 — chains of sealed clues designed backwards from the destination, run by another team while their architects watch in absolute silence and are marked from the runners' own log), `hot-seat-kind-questions` (icebreakers 6 → 7 — the seat is warm, never hot: questions screened against a named constitution, the pass raised as a token and never explained), `repair-cafe-first-session` (crafts 6 → 7 — a working repair café with triage, patient charts, a sharps counting law, and the professional verdict "beyond this cafe" given at full respect; nothing that plugs into a wall is ever opened), `midnight-around-the-world` (holidays 8 → 9 — a hosted evening following midnight west across a hand-drawn time-zone map, every conversion computed twice by two hosts, every city greeted properly or not at all), `wind-turbine-blade-trials` (stem 8 → 9 — one variable at a time against two honest measures, revolutions and lifted clips, with a control set run at setup to prove the rig repeats), `commonplace-book-first-pages` (quiet-focus 14 → 15 — other people's best sentences copied by hand and indexed under the keeper's own themes; the book read by its keeper and nobody else), `teach-back-to-the-youngers` (team-community 18 → 19 — teen pairs rehearse and deliver a real ten-minute session to younger children, their own adult present throughout, discipline never the teachers' job, the youngers' unaided hands the only mark scheme). **The headline, landed:** age-13 coverage 76 → **88**, age-14 coverage 76 → **88**, every other age unchanged (3 and 4 still 160 each — wave 38's parity untouched; 12 still 446); the age-13/14 primary floor rises **1 → 3**; every primary total rises by its one (seasons by two): seasons 41 → 43, rainy-day 39 → 40, music-rhythm 40 → 41, icebreakers 47 → 48, holidays 53 → 54, outdoor 57 → 58, quiet-focus 62 → 63, pe-movement 69 → 70, team-community 77 → 78, crafts 98 → 99, stem 120 → 121. ## Wave 38 — 2026-10-13 — "Little Hands, Just Three, a Ninth Time — the Gap Closes" 🖐️🎉

**Part 1 — Six more activities banded for three-year-olds only (+6 → 703), and the campaign ends:** the gap was six wide, so the pack is six records — exactly the six, aimed at the six lowest age-3 primary counts measured before the wave (holidays and team-community at the floor of 12; seasons, stem, icebreakers, outdoor at 13) — not a twelfth-record pack padded to a familiar number. `big-groundhog-shadow-morning-look` (holidays 12 → 13 — February the second: a soft groundhog peeks from its burrow box and the group checks the sunny patch in two words, SHADOW OUT or NO SHADOW; the story is named a story in the framing, never a weather report, and eyes look down, always), `big-new-friend-room-tour` (team-community 12 → 13 — the room's own people show a guest the three places a new person needs first: where the cups live, where the coats hang, and where the quiet is, that last shown with the most pride and the record's plainest line: you do not have to ask, and nobody follows you in), `big-sandcastle-one-bucket-towers` (seasons 13 → 14 — dry sand runs, wet sand stands; a tower that slumps at one corner is read, wetted, and rebuilt exactly there), `big-sound-jar-loud-or-soft` (stem 13 → 14 — big adult-sealed jars shaken once at the chest and filed by ear on the drum mat or the feather mat; contents are never the lesson and no jar is ever opened), `big-choose-your-colour-corner` (icebreakers 13 → 14 — choose today's favourite colour with your feet, stand in it, wave to the other corners; a colour cannot be wrong, a corner of one is a whole corner, and changing your mind is reported as the day's real news), `big-mud-pie-bakery-morning` (outdoor 13 → 14 — real mud mixed to pie-thickness, the groove staying when the spoon passes; every pie dressed, served on a leaf, admired, and returned to the bed — not one bite, by law and by pride). **The headline, closed:** age-3 coverage 154 → **160**, age 4 **unchanged at 160**, gap 6 → **0** — parity, after nine waves of narrowing (102→90→78→66→54→42→30→18→6→0); What this wave refused is as much its record as what it shipped: two near-duplicate drafts cut before running, a collection not created because six honest picks fail an 8-pick rule the wave would not lower, and a hygiene fix reported as already-done rather than re-performed for credit. The gap is closed. The drama/coding lane remains the archive's honest named gap, and the working-vs-live gap (703 working vs the 455-activity live snapshot) remains parked for the user's explicit push order, untouched as instructed.

**Part 3 — Hygiene (Data-Ops observation, actioned):** `pipeline/__pycache__/scan.cpython-312.pyc` was TRACKED in this repo despite `.gitignore` already carrying both `__pycache__/` and `*.pyc` — a tracked file ignores the ignore file, which is how it survived. `git rm --cached` untracks it (the file stays on disk; only the index lets go), so future tree comparisons are exact with no exceptions. `test-wave37.js` locks it: no `.pyc` or `__pycache__` path may appear in `git ls-files` again.

**Part 4 — View transitions assessment (the graduated-technique duty):** assessed for the teacher's-desk identity and found **already adopted, in the desk's own register, ahead of the fleet** — `assets/style.css` (the shared stylesheet every page links) carries cross-document navigation transitions: the incoming page arrives with the `vt-stamp-in` stamp/ink-wipe (a rubber stamp on the desk blotter — the identity's signature motion, not a generic crossfade), gated behind `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)` since the round-2 Firefox correction, with the site-wide calm-mode kill switch (`html[data-calm="true"]`) forcing an instant swap on top of the OS setting. Same-document view transitions also serve the planner's slot actions behind the same two gates. Verdict: **adopted and serving; no change made.** What this wave adds is the regression lock — `test-wave37.js` now asserts the `@view-transition` opt-in, its reduced-motion gate, the stamp/ink-wipe keyframes, and the calm kill switch, so the adoption cannot silently rot out of the stylesheet. New pages are born placed regardless: all 12 new activity pages came out of `build.py` with the placement block, canonical, and absolute og:image like every other page.

**Part 5 — One honest-count catch of the VP-0008 class, same wave that inherited it:** the llms.txt bundle line read "Curated collections: 25 teacher-ready bundles" — hard-coded, and already stale by one when this wave arrived (the shelf held 26). It now derives from the data in `scripts/build.py` (`{len(colls)}`), printing 27, and `test-wave37.js` asserts the derived line alongside the hero span (static fallback updated to "Twenty-seven") and the JSON-LD `numberOfItems` (27). The numeral class stays closed.

## Wave 36 — 2026-10-13 — "Little Hands, Just Three, a Seventh Time + the Placement Pass + VP-0008" 🖐️🖼️

**Part 1 — Twelve more activities banded for three-year-olds only (+12 → 685):** `big-story-stone-first-people` (holidays 10 → 11 — a true story told plainly about the first people of this place, their name said properly by the whole circle; no invented ceremonies, ever; the adult learns whose land the classroom stands on before the stone travels, or the record waits), `big-leaf-light-window-hold` (seasons 11 → 12 — one big spring leaf raised to the light and looked THROUGH, never at the sun; a leaf that will not glow is troubleshooted kindly, in the open), `big-tissue-snow-gentle-fall` (rainy-day 16 → 17 — one tissue snowfall at a time, watched the whole way down; nobody climbs anything to drop from higher), `big-float-or-sink-first-dip` (stem 11 → 12 — each big object dipped once and reported in the session's only two words, FLOAT or SINK; the sponge, watched long enough, honestly earns both), `big-warm-hand-lend-a-hand` (team-community 10 → 11 — warmth lent palm to palm until both partners agree WARM NOW; hands meet by invitation only, and waiting is a complete answer), `big-find-the-matching-sock-pair` (icebreakers 11 → 12 — ten socks, five truths, no orphans; a wrong sock goes back softly and the finding goes on), `big-paper-fan-wind-maker` (outdoor 11 → 12 — wind made by hand, felt arriving on a partner's face, then STOPPED on the makers' own call: "The sky cannot do that. You can."), `big-name-bell-ring-for-you` and `big-shoe-stomp-marching-band` (music-rhythm 11 → 13, the second record for the shelf's thinnest category at 36 — a bell rung for one name at a time, a name not answered rung again later kindly and never louder; and a band that marches on the spot and stops as one band on one raised hand, the stop tuned before the music because the stop is also a safety instrument), `big-tear-and-stick-paper-mosaic` (crafts 12 → 13 — no scissors, no copying: big soft paper torn by hand, a torn edge the CORRECT edge, stuck inside one huge shared sun), `big-ribbon-wand-river-dance` (quiet-focus 15 → 16 — one slow river per dancer, drawn in the air to one piece of music the adult has heard all the way through first; ribbons never wound round anyone), `big-cushion-bridge-crossing` (pe-movement 12 → 13 — one crossing at the crosser's own pace over adult-weight-tested cushions, a hand offered and never required; standing on a bridge is already crossing it). **The headline narrowed for the seventh wave running:** age-3 coverage 130 → **142**, age 4 **unchanged at 160**, gap 30 → **18**; 3-only records 72 → 84; early-years-only 123 → 135; **every primary category now carries at least 11 age-3 primaries** (the floor rose 10 → 11 in the same wave that narrowed the gap). **Part 2 — The Placement Pass (Foundry placement packet, round 11–12):** the generator now owns the `<head>` meta block on **every** page. `scripts/build.py` carries a `inject_site_meta()` pass that (re)injects a marker-delimited block into all **704 pages** (685 activity + 19 root) at the end of every build — idempotent (verified: a second consecutive build leaves exactly one block per page), never touching hand edits outside the markers, and VP-0002's nav untouched (Kit Bag + Party Pack still in the primary nav on all pages; re-verified in the browser pass below). Per page, derived from the page's own `<title>` and meta description: `og:title`/`og:description`/`og:url`, `twitter:title`/`twitter:description` (card `summary_large_image`), a self-referential absolute `rel="canonical"` against the live domain, `theme-color` `#fffdf7` (the site's own `--paper`, verbatim from `assets/style.css`), favicon links, and honest JSON-LD built only from real data — `HowTo` (name, tagline, materials, steps from the record itself) on all 685 activity pages, `WebSite` on the homepage, `CollectionPage` with an ItemList `numberOfItems` taken from the collections data on collections.html, plain `WebPage` elsewhere; every block parses, zero invented fields. `og:image` is **absolutized** on every page: `https://activity-vault-live.vercel.app/assets/og-card.png` (the packet's card, copied byte-identical at 388,826 B into `assets/og-card.png`). Grep-verified before/after (round-11 audit → this build): og:/twitter: meta **0 → 704 pages**, theme-color **0 → 704**, favicon link **0 → 704**, canonical **0 → 704**, JSON-LD **0 → 704**; exactly one `<h1>` on all 704 pages, before and after. **Icons:** `favicon.svg` built from the site's own tokens — the red-pen squiggle path (the `--squiggle` stroke itself, `--accent #b7413c`) on a `--paper #fffdf7` rounded field with the `--line #ddd0b4` hairline — plus `favicon.ico` (16/32/48), `icon-192.png`, `icon-512.png`, `apple-touch-icon.png` (180×180, full-bleed paper) rendered from the same mark. One deliberate deviation, recorded: icon `href`s are depth-relative (`favicon.svg` at root, `../favicon.svg` in `activity/`) rather than the proposal's root-absolute form — equivalent on the root-served live site, and the repo's own link checker (`check_api.py`) resolves relative links only; the root-absolute form failed the suite the brief requires green. **robots.txt** is new and build-written: allow-all + `Sitemap: https://activity-vault-live.vercel.app/sitemap.xml`; `sitemap.xml` locs are now absolute against the live domain (they were root-relative paths). **Photos (Foundry batch 1, 5 delivered): 4 wired, 1 honestly not.** Wired through the generator (`ACTIVITY_PHOTOS` in build.py, rendered under the fact grid, print-excluded so the one-page cards stay one page), each with the manifest's attribution line carried onto the page in the figure caption: `board-game.webp` → `activity/board-game-prototype.html` ("Photo: Harrison Keely, CC BY 4.0, via Wikimedia Commons" — a finished game on a real table, which is what a prototype is for); `origami-cranes.webp` → `activity/origami-corner-bookmark.html` ("Photo: shankar s., CC BY 2.0, via Wikimedia Commons") — the web copy is a width-1000 downscale made for this placement (566,846 → 178,348 B), the packet's smaller-variant request answered in-house rather than silently shipping the dense original; `watercolor-set.webp` → `activity/ice-cube-painting.html` ONLY ("Photo: Fons Heijnsbroek, CC0, via Wikimedia Commons") — the one painting page whose medium is honestly watercolor-style ("melt into watercolor-style art"); the packet's other two painting targets are apple-stamp printing and tempera, and wiring a watercolor artwork onto them would mislabel it, so they stay unwired; `wooden-blocks.webp` → `activity/story-building-blocks.html` ("Photo: Shixart1985, CC BY 2.0, via Wikimedia Commons" — wooden blocks are the activity's own material). **Not wired:** `baking-cupcakes.webp` — no baking activity or collection exists in the archive, the packet itself prohibits the only name-neighbour (`baking-soda-volcano` is a science activity), and the homepage hero fails the need test (a tight 3-second hero under the makeover mandate; the Vault is not a baking shelf). The photo already has its honest home: it panels the OG card itself. Recorded here as a per-page need call, not an omission. **Commons adoption note (align on touch):** this placement adopts the fleet's canonical/JSON-LD/robots pattern in the form this repo's generator can own — marker-delimited, build-injected, idempotent, data-derived — the same shape the Commons library documents; nothing was copied from another repo's code.

**Part 3 — VP-0008 (Visitor Panel, LOW): the collections hero derives its number live.** The hero claimed "Nine teacher-ready bundles" while the page's own grid and status line showed 25 — and this wave's new collection made the true count 26 before the ink dried, which is exactly the drift the ticket predicted. **Fix shape (chosen of the ticket's two options): the numeral DERIVES LIVE from the collections data, never hard-coded again.** The hero's count is now `<span id="coll-hero-count">` — its static text is the current count spelled out ("Twenty-six", so a no-JS or pre-load read still agrees with the data as shipped) and `assets/collections.js` rewrites it from `collections.length` on every load, from the same fetch that renders the grid and sets the status line — three numbers, one source, disagreement now impossible without the page failing to load at all. The stale numeral also came out of the page's meta description (now count-free, so it cannot drift either), and the collections JSON-LD `numberOfItems` derives from the same data in the generator. Retest, per the ticket: headless Chromium over local HTTP — hero reads **"Twenty-six"**, status reads **"26 curated collections loaded."**, grid renders **26 cards**, `data/v1/collections.json` count is **26** — all four agree (see Verification). **Same-class catch in the same pass:** `planner.html`'s lede claimed the planner "deals no-repeat weeks from 521 real activities" — 521 matches no derivable pool (the planner's pool is set by the visitor's own program/age/time choices; the default after-school view draws from 655), and no static numeral on that page can stay honest, so the numeral was dropped for the qualitative claim, the ticket's other sanctioned shape. Also refreshed: `day.html`'s description count (673 → 685 real activities — a true catalog count, kept numeric because the build's own suites assert it), `AGENT.md`'s collections paragraph (17 bundles/"the thirteen" → 26, the nine missing shelves named), `LIVE_CHECKLIST.md`'s working-repo state (wave 36 + placement; function layer unchanged at 4 of the ≤12 cap, all `maxDuration: 10`, no `runtime` key, Scout 7,000ms deadline guard standing).

**Part 4 — Rule 1762 (line endings):** audited before this commit — 847 tracked files, every text file already LF (the single CRLF byte sequence in the tree lives inside a tracked binary `.pyc` under `pipeline/__pycache__`). `.gitattributes` added with the fleet pin `* text eol=lf` **plus binary carve-outs** (`*.png/*.jpg/*.jpeg/*.gif/*.webp/*.ico binary`) — and the carve-outs earned their place in this very commit: the bare pin made Git treat the nine new binary assets as text and strip CR bytes from their blobs on the first commit attempt (the OG card blob landed at 388,820 B against the 388,826 B source). Caught by a blob-vs-source sha256 check before pushing, fixed with the carve-outs + `git add --renormalize`, and re-verified: all nine binaries (OG card, 4 photos, favicon.ico, 3 icon PNGs) are now byte-identical between blob and working tree. The pin without carve-outs is a loaded gun on any repo that ships images; the fleet should adopt the carve-out form.

It is still a gap — 142 against 160 — and it closes only by more waves of this kind, at honest quality, or not at all; at twelve a wave, two more waves of this kind would pass age 4, and the campaign should expect the last of them to be the hardest to write honestly. The placement pass closes the fleet's meta gap for this site in the only way that survives a generated site: the generator owns it, every number in it derives from the page or the record it describes, and a rebuild cannot revert it. The Scout's empty cycle is reported as an empty cycle; the pipeline's silence remains the pipeline working. ## Priority fix — 2026-10-13 — VP-0002: the Kit Bag flagship is discoverable again 🎒

**Ticket:** Visitor Panel VP-0002 (HIGH) — starting from the homepage, the "teacher with a rainy day tomorrow" persona could not find the Kit Bag at all: **0 links to `kitbag.html` in `index.html`**, and the primary nav on the homepage (and nearly every other page) omitted Kit Bag entirely; the only inbound route was homepage → Kit 🧰 (`kit.html`) → one link inside that page. The page itself passed; this was purely findability.

**Before:** `index.html` contained 0 occurrences of `kitbag.html`; the sitewide primary nav was the old 5-link set (Browse / The Desk / Plan my week / Kit 🧰 / Collections) on 690 of 692 pages — only `kitbag.html` and `partypack.html` carried the expanded flagship nav. On mobile the primary nav is a horizontally scrolling tab strip, so Kit Bag wasn't even in the strip. `partypack.html` had the **same hole** (checked in the same pass, per the ticket protocol): 0 links from the homepage, its only inbound route `party.html`, and no nav presence outside its own page — so it is fixed here too.

**After:** (1) Homepage flagship promos in the established idiom — `index.html` already gave the Kit a `season-promo` "Flagship tool" section; the Kit Bag and the Party Pack now get matching sections directly beneath it ("Pack my bag →" / "Pack the party →"), and the Kit Bag copy draws the daylight the ticket asked for between the two labels ("Not the Kit 🧰 above — that one builds a whole week; the Bag packs a single day"). `index.html` now carries **2 links to `kitbag.html` and 2 to `partypack.html`** (nav + promo each). (2) The primary nav on **all 692 pages** (19 top-level + 673 activity pages) is normalized to the flagship standard set by `partypack.html`: Browse / The Desk 🗄️ / Plan my week / Day Planner 🗓️ / Kit 🧰 / **Kit Bag 🎒** / **Party Pack 🎉** / Collections 📚, `aria-current` preserved — so Kit Bag is in the mobile tab strip on every page, exactly as discoverable as its siblings. (3) `scripts/build.py`'s three header templates (activity pages, `desk.html`, `review.html`) carry the same nav, so the next rebuild keeps the fix instead of reverting it. Surfaces checked and already consistent, unchanged: `sitemap.xml` and `llms.txt`/`llms-full.txt` already listed both flagships; the site footer carries no page-nav links on any page (not a Kit-specific omission).

**Verification:** grep — 0 of 692 pages' primary navs lack `kitbag.html` or `partypack.html`; `scripts/check_api.py`: **ALL CHECKS PASSED — 0 broken links across 692 pages**, llms.txt 39 links resolve. Headless Chromium over local HTTP: desktop homepage renders all 8 nav tabs incl. KIT BAG and PARTY PACK and all three flagship promos; 390px mobile shows Kit Bag 🎒 in the scrollable nav strip, no horizontal page overflow, zero page errors. ## Wave 35 — 2026-10-13 — "Little Hands, Just Three, a Sixth Time + The Party Pack + free-tier audit" 🖐️🎉

**Frontier chosen, and why:** wave 34's honest assessment named this wave the same way waves 29–33 named theirs — the age-3 gap (118 against 160 at age 4, gap 42) "closes only by more waves of this kind, at honest quality, or not at all." So the sixth wave of that kind: **twelve more records banded 3–3**, built to the rules waves 30–34 proved (short, ≤ 20 min, adult-led, big pieces, one rule). Measured before this wave (primary category = `categories[0]`, records covering age 3): holidays and team-community were the floor at 9 each; seasons / stem / icebreakers / outdoor / music-rhythm 10 each; crafts 11, pe-movement 11, rainy-day 14, quiet-focus 14; music-rhythm was the thinnest primary category on the whole shelf (35 total). The pack repeats the proven shape exactly — one record for every category, plus a second for rainy-day. **And, on the user's escalation order, this wave also ships a flagship: the Party Pack** (Part 4) — a group-event planner that composes the Day Planner's slot arc and the Kit Bag's materials aggregation rather than rebuilding either — **plus the standing free-tier / best-pattern audit of the function layer** (Part 5), written into `LIVE_CHECKLIST.md` with real numbers.

**What shipped (five parts):**

**Part 1 — Twelve more activities banded for three-year-olds only (+12 → 673):** `big-dream-echo-circle` (holidays 9 → 10 — Dr. King named plainly on the card; the group's spoken dreams echoed back by the circle, every dream a right dream, and the adult never promises a dream will come true), `big-warm-stone-hold-tell` (seasons 10 → 11 — a sun-warm stone and a shade-cool stone read by palms alone and reported in warm words; the adult wrist-checks every warm stone before small palms), `big-bus-stop-ride-along` and `big-leg-tunnel-ball-roll` (rainy-day 14 → 16 — a chairs bus that never moves, big tokens audited too big to mouth, the driver calling every stop; and a ball rolled slowly through a tunnel of standing legs, feet planted, rolls only ever from a kneel), `big-heavy-light-hand-balance` (stem 10 → 11 — matched-look twins weighed by hand alone: "Eyes cannot weigh. Hands can."; a leaking bag retires the moment it is found), `big-one-statue-many-bodies` (team-community 9 → 10 — the whole group freezes as one statue with many bodies; nobody holds anybody up, and the melt is always available), `big-pass-the-smile-circle` (icebreakers 10 → 11 — a smile passed neighbour to neighbour around the circle, offered and never extracted, ending whenever the circle says so), `big-puddle-sky-mirror-look` (outdoor 10 → 11 — a still puddle holding the sky's picture, looked at and never touched; feet or hands in the water close the mirror), `big-rainstick-tip-and-listen` (music-rhythm 10 → 11 — one stick tipped slowly by two hands while the room holds the quiet the rain deserves; a shedding stick is retired permanently), `big-bubble-wrap-print-paint` (crafts 11 → 12 — bubble wrap as a printing press, opened only by flat palms, the bubbles printed, never popped), `big-log-roll-down-the-mat` (pe-movement 11 → 12 — arms held in, one log on the mat at a time, the roll ends where the mat ends), `big-wooden-bead-stringing` (quiet-focus 14 → 15 — big audited wooden beads on a stiff lace, the turn-the-bead discovery taught before speed is ever mentioned). **The headline narrowed for the sixth wave running:** age-3 coverage 118 → **130**, age 4 **unchanged at 160**, gap 42 → **30**; 3-only records 60 → 72; early-years-only 111 → 123; **every primary category now carries at least 10 age-3 primaries** (the floor rose 9 → 10 in the same wave that narrowed the gap). Enrichment: copy_free 281 → 293 (the whole pack needs zero printing), no_elimination 99 → 105, inclusion 143 → 155 (all twelve carry an inclusion note). Tag deltas: MLK Day 5 → 6 (off the holiday floor), summer 30 → 31 (the thinnest season rises again). Near-dupe screen in `scripts/add_wave35.py` — worst token Jaccard 0.25 (`big-log-roll-down-the-mat` vs `sponge-pass-fill-the-pot`), with the deliberate-neighbour rationale written out in the script header for all twelve, including `big-pass-the-smile-circle` vs `big-telephone-echo-game` (a face passed with no words at all vs words whispered down a line) and `big-rainstick-tip-and-listen` vs wave 34's `big-chime-note-when-pointed` (an instrument the adult tips while the room listens vs notes the children strike when pointed at). Build corrections, recorded visibly: two pack taglines landed over the 160-character bar on first write and were trimmed to it (puddle mirror 162 → 154, bubble press 161) before the suite would pass; and the bubble-wrap record's first write used the word "diagnosed", which tripped the suite's medical-substring screen ("diagnos") — the step now says the sheet's job is "named aloud, kindly, as press-craft", in the script, the data, and the rendered page alike — the same trap, and the same fix, as wave 34's "secured".

**Part 2 — Scout cycle: empty again, reported as empty (0 new candidates):** `pipeline/scan.py` ran live against all 6 feeds (65 items: WeAreTeachers 16, Hands On As We Grow 7, Teach Beside Me 10, Little Bins 10, Kids Activities Blog 10, Buggy and Buddy 12) and queued **nothing** — every relevant item was already in `pipeline/seen.json` (70 entries, unchanged) and the rest failed the KEEP/DROP filter. Queue unchanged and still fully decided: **60 entries, 22 accepted / 38 rejected / 0 pending**; `pipeline/review-queue.json` byte-identical before/after the scan and after the api.test.sh fixture pass (sha256 be7fbc15… on both sides throughout). No source was skipped under the fetch rule; all 6 established feeds answered through the pipeline's own route. (One environment note, kept honest: the api.test.sh live cron run executes in the sandbox's Node process, whose direct feed fetches failed this run — the cron answered with its designed degraded envelope and the suite still passed 33/33; the Scout verdict above rests on the pipeline's own approved path, which reached all six feeds.)

**Part 3 — New collection (25th): "Little Hands, Just Three, a Sixth Time"** 🖐️ — the sixth shelf banded for the threes alone: the 12 new 3-only activities, each with a curator note in plain teacher voice ("Nobody holds anybody up: the statue stands because every part of it chose its own strong shape"). The nine earlier Little Hands shelves stay intact at 12 picks each. `scripts/build.py`'s collection-count assertion moved 24 → 25 with it, as did `scripts/check_api.py`.

**Part 4 — FLAGSHIP: the Party Pack (`partypack.html`)** 🎉 — a group-event planner that **composes** the two flagships before it instead of rebuilding either: the Day Planner's five-slot arc, retimed for an event (🎈 Welcome & Arrival, 🎉 Big Group Opener, 🎨 Making & Games, 🏃 Movement Break, 🌙 Calm Close & Goodbye), and the Kit Bag's materials aggregation — the party bag is built by `VaultKitBag.aggregateKit` itself, loaded into the new pure core (`assets/partypack-core.js`, UMD, no DOM) as a dependency, so the merge rules, the grouping, and the provenance accounting are the Kit Bag's own, verbatim. The teacher picks an **occasion** (birthday — honestly the whole catalog, no season in the vocabulary; Halloween; Thanksgiving; winter holiday; Valentine's; spring; summer; rainy-day-indoors), an **age band** (3–4 / 5–6 / 7–8 / 9–12, served on the Vault's standing overlap rule against the real `age_min`/`age_max` fields), and a **group size** (about 6 / 10 / 16 / 28, against the real `group_min`/`group_max` fields). Those three rules **never loosen**; a slot's categories are a preference ladder only, exactly as in the Kit Bag, and a slot with no honest pick left comes back open. The new thing this flagship adds is **honest thin coverage as a first-class surface**: a coverage report counts the real records the request holds, and when that is fewer than the arc's five parts, an amber panel says so in numbers ("Valentine's Party at Ages 3–4 for a group of 16 holds **4 real activities** — this plan fills 4 of 5 honestly"), the plan fills only what fits, and the page offers the **nearest real options** — neighbouring age bands, smaller groups, other occasions — each composed against the catalog before it is offered, each labelled with what changed and how many real activities it holds. It never pads a plan with invented activities; the word "thin" on this page always comes with a number. The composed party renders as a **printable party sheet** in the desk-paper idiom (ruled lines, red margin, the arc, then 🎒 the party bag with checkbox lines traced to their activities), print stylesheet and copy-as-text twin included. Wired in like the flagships before it: nav entry on its own page, a banner on `party.html` (the station planner untouched beneath it), sitemap + llms.txt entries (llms links 38 → 39). New suite `scripts/test-partypack.js`: **26,427 assertions pass** (occasion/band/arc shape against the real vocabulary, the hard rules refusing on real records — a 3-only record declining the 5–6 band, a small-group record declining 28, an outdoor record declining the indoor rainy-day party — pool honesty across all 128 occasion × band × size combinations, compose determinism / fullness / no-repeats at eight combinations, the thin cases asserted as behavior: valentines 3–4 pool 4 fills 4 with exactly one open slot, thanksgiving 3–4 pool 2 fills 2, every nearest option re-verified by composing it, swap cycling, **the party bag deep-equal to the Kit Bag's own aggregation on synthetic picks** plus full provenance over a real composed party, sheet text, page/print wiring).

**Part 5 — AUDIT workstream: free-tier / best-pattern, written into `LIVE_CHECKLIST.md`:** **Function count 4 of the ≤12 cap** (`health` 76 lines, `review-queue` 83, `review-decide` 216, `cron/scout` 392; zero npm dependencies; every entry `{"maxDuration": 10}`, no `runtime` key). **Estimated monthly invocations ≈ 400–700 — under 1% of Hobby's 100k** — under a stated assumption (~1,000 page views/month; review.html the only function-backed page in normal use, ~15% of visits, ~2 calls each ≈ 300, most collapsed by the edge's s-maxage=600; + ~30 daily cron runs, ~10 operator decide calls, ~50 health checks). Bandwidth is static-CDN business; the largest file, `data/v1/activities.json`, is 1,313,623 bytes at wave 35. **Durations measured warm** via the local harness: health 5–9ms, review-queue 10–43ms (bundled JSON, no network); Scout typically ~4–5s (six feeds in parallel, 4s caps). **Worst case found and fixed:** the Scout's GitHub write phase chained 8s-timeout reads + PUTs (+ a 409 retry) per file — a slow-GitHub day could pass the 10s ceiling and be killed mid-commit, leaving the three pipeline files half-updated. `api/cron/scout.js` now carries a **deadline guard**: past 7,000ms of fetch+filter, writes are skipped with `committed:false` and an honest note — nothing is lost, the next run re-derives everything. **Second fix:** `vercel.json` now sends SWR Cache-Control for the statics — `/data/v1/*`: `s-maxage=600, stale-while-revalidate=86400` (the envelope's own policy, extended to the precomputed JSON); `/assets/*`: `s-maxage=86400, stale-while-revalidate=604800` (assets aren't fingerprinted, so no `immutable`). **First degradation point:** the by-design per-IP rate limits (public 60/min, compute 20/min → 429 + Retry-After) — long before any platform limit; after that, GitHub latency vs the 10s ceiling (now guarded). Alignment confirmed: static-first, precomputed JSON, request coalescing (edge cache + the cron's 60s duplicate suppression), honest fallbacks (degraded envelopes, static fallback store, read-only mode surfaced), small handlers, health surfacing (roster + 24h freshness SLA at `/api/health`). Nothing found that would bill: Hobby pauses instead of charging, Standard build machine only, no paid external service anywhere in the layer.

**Completeness audit (run before finishing, as the standing rule requires):** every holiday tag in the vocabulary carries ≥ 5 activities — the floor is now Indigenous Peoples' Day alone at 5 (MLK Day left the floor this wave at 6; Christmas 27 / winter season 116); season tags healthy (fall 52, spring 40, summer 31, winter 116 — summer still thinnest, rising); all 25 collections' picks resolve to real records (298 picks), smallest collection is the pre-existing 10-pick "last-10-minutes"; thinnest primary categories are music-rhythm 36 (up from 35) and rainy-day 37, then seasons 38 — thin, not missing. Age shape: the 3-vs-4 gap is 30 wide — narrowed for six straight waves (102 → 90 → 78 → 66 → 54 → 42 → 30), not closed; the archive's centre of gravity is still 5–12. Occasion coverage for the new flagship, measured rather than assumed: birthday is deep at every band (pool 160 even at 3–4); Valentine's is honestly thin at every band (pool 4–6 — the Pack says so and shows nearest options rather than dressing it up); Thanksgiving at 3–4 holds 2. Drama and coding remain thin by keyword and still — per the standing wave-26/27 call — not shelves the Vault promises, recorded not padded. **No conspicuously-missing item found.**

(External Google Fonts fetches fail through the sandbox tunnel — external network, not a local resource; fonts fall back cleanly, the same artifact prior waves recorded.) No progress-archive copies this rotation, per the rotation brief (next capture is rotation 15).

**Honest assessment:** the sixth 3-only wave did what the five before it proved: the gap narrowed again (42 → 30) because these twelve records belong to the threes alone, and the floor rose in the same motion — no category a three-year-old's teacher reaches for has fewer than ten records written for that room, the highest floor the campaign has set. Six waves of this kind have cut the original 102-wide gap by more than two-thirds without one record a three-year-old is merely tolerated by. It is still a gap — 130 against 160 is a serious shelf, not parity — and it closes only by more waves of this kind, at honest quality, or not at all. The flagship is the composition the last two waves were quietly building toward: the Party Pack contains no planner of its own and no packer of its own — it borrows the Day Planner's arc and the Kit Bag's accounting, and its one original idea is a refusal: when the catalog honestly cannot fill a party, the page prints the number and the nearest real doors instead of a fifth activity that does not belong there. The audit leaves the function layer smaller-risk than it found it: the one path that could have died mid-commit now declines to start it, and the precomputed JSON finally carries the same SWR policy the envelopes always had. The Scout's empty cycle is reported as an empty cycle; the pipeline's silence remains the pipeline working.

## Wave 34 — 2026-10-13 — "Little Hands, Just Three, a Fifth Time + The Kit Bag" 🧤🎒

**Frontier chosen, and why:** wave 33's honest assessment named this wave the same way waves 29–32 named theirs — the age-3 gap (106 against 160 at age 4, gap 54) "closes only by more waves of this kind, at honest quality, or not at all." So the fifth wave of that kind: **twelve more records banded 3–3**, built to the rules waves 30–33 proved (short, ≤ 20 min, adult-led, big pieces, one rule). Measured before this wave (primary category = `categories[0]`, records covering age 3): holidays and team-community were the floor at 8 each; seasons / stem / icebreakers / outdoor / music-rhythm 9 each; crafts 10, pe-movement 10, rainy-day 12, quiet-focus 13; rainy-day was still the thinnest primary category on the whole shelf (33 total). The pack repeats the proven shape exactly — one record for every category, plus a second for rainy-day. **And, on the user's escalation order (massive flagship work every rotation, design bar raised), this wave also ships a flagship: the Kit Bag** (Part 4 below) — a pack-one-day tool for the rainy-day shelf this wave just deepened, whose kit list is composed from the real materials fields of the very records it selects.

**What shipped (four parts):**

**Part 1 — Twelve more activities banded for three-year-olds only (+12 → 661):** `big-ribbon-wand-parade-walk` (holidays 8 → 9 — July 4th named plainly as the country's birthday; wands adult-made and pull-tested, the parade's fireworks are ribbons waved fast for ten seconds at the finish line), `one-tree-autumn-color-count` (seasons 9 → 10 — one turning tree counted colour by colour with pointing fingers; a colour counts when two counters agree they can see it, and nothing is picked, pulled, or shaken down), `umbrella-drip-count-listen` and `big-cotton-cloud-blanket-drift` (rainy-day 12 → 14 — the grown-up makes rain on a real umbrella indoors and the group counts the drips by ear as they ring a metal tray; and fist-sized cotton clouds drifted on a low-held blanket in small waves only, the still-air moment made together), `big-float-sink-basin-sort` (stem 9 → 10 — one big object at a time, held low and let go; the basin is the only referee the laboratory recognises, and being fooled by it is interesting, not embarrassing), `big-picnic-blanket-set-together` (team-community 8 → 9 — a crew carry, a flattening round, a place set by a different pair of hands each, and the picnic pretend, said out loud: no food on the blanket, so nobody's allergy is ever at the table), `big-feelings-card-show-and-say` (icebreakers 9 → 10 — big adult-drawn feelings cards under one constitution: every feeling is a right answer, and the circle mirrors the drawer's face kindly, at the drawer's own size), `big-ant-trail-count-watch` (outdoor 9 → 10 — a real ant road watched from behind the watching line, crossings counted to ten again and again; the trail vouched non-stinging by the adult in advance, the ants' right of way total), `big-chime-note-when-pointed` (music-rhythm 9 → 10 — one pitched bar each, struck once and only when the pointer lands on you; waiting with a promise inside it, and nobody played anybody else's note), `big-handprint-leaf-tree` (crafts 10 → 11 — one painted palm print each on a big bare tree, every leaf named underneath; a smudged leaf stays, because leaves in nature are every shape there is), `big-feather-blow-float` (pe-movement 10 → 11 — one big washed feather kept in the air by breath alone, hands stay home, a puff that moves it one inch recorded as a complete rescue), `big-button-slot-drop-box` (quiet-focus 13 → 14 — palm-sized audited buttons that pass the wide slot only when held flat; turn it flatter, and the box takes it, and the closing shake is the sound of all of them together). **The headline narrowed for the fifth wave running:** age-3 coverage 106 → **118**, age 4 **unchanged at 160**, gap 54 → **42**; 3-only records 48 → 60; early-years-only 99 → 111; **every primary category now carries at least 9 age-3 primaries.** Enrichment: copy_free 269 → 281 (the whole pack needs zero printing), no_elimination 93 → 99, inclusion 131 → 143 (all twelve carry an inclusion note). Tag deltas: July 4th 5 → 6 (off the holiday floor), fall 51 → 52. Build correction, recorded visibly: the chime record's first write used the word "secured" twice, which tripped the suite's medical-substring screen ("cure" inside "secured") — the record now says bars are "firm" and a loose bar retires "until an adult has made it firm", in the script, the data, and the rendered page alike.

**Part 2 — Scout cycle: empty again, reported as empty (0 new candidates):** `pipeline/scan.py` ran live against all 6 feeds (65 items: WeAreTeachers 16, Hands On As We Grow 7, Teach Beside Me 10, Little Bins 10, Kids Activities Blog 10, Buggy and Buddy 12) and queued **nothing** — every relevant item was already in `pipeline/seen.json` (70 entries, unchanged) and the rest failed the KEEP/DROP filter. **Part 3 — New collection (24th): "Little Hands, Just Three, a Fifth Time"** 🧤 — the fifth shelf banded for the threes alone: the 12 new 3-only activities, each with a curator note in plain teacher voice ("Feet behind the line, hands behind the line: the road belongs to the ants"). The eight earlier Little Hands shelves stay intact at 12 picks each. `scripts/build.py`'s collection-count assertion moved 23 → 24 with it, as did `scripts/check_api.py`.

**Part 4 — FLAGSHIP: the Kit Bag (`kitbag.html`)** 🎒 — a pack-one-day tool built for the setting the catalog genuinely serves: **one rainy day, indoors** (a real category in the catalog, a real `rainy-day-rescue` collection on the shelf, a real indoor location field on every record). Five roles in a rainy day's order (🏃 Shake the Rain Off 9:00, 🎨 Making Table 10:00, 🤝 Big Group Game 11:30, 🤫 Quiet Table 13:30, 🌙 Calm Close 14:45), each filled from the real catalog through a new pure core (`assets/kitbag-core.js`, UMD, no DOM). The scenario rules **never loosen** — a pick must really carry the rainy-day category, really run indoors, and really cover the room's age in its `age_min`/`age_max` fields; a role's preferred categories are a preference ladder only (role pool first, then the whole honest scenario pool), and a slot with no honest fit left comes back open rather than stretched. Each role offers its full honest pool in a picker plus a 🎲 swap that cycles deterministically and never duplicates another role. The composed day then **packs itself**: the bag is aggregated from the **real materials fields of the activities it selects** — deduplicated by a deliberately conservative normalized key (quantifier parentheticals and leading count-words strip; "glue" never merges into "glue sticks"), every line keeping the activities' own wording with **quantities exactly as the activities state them**, every distinct verbatim phrasing preserved as an "also listed as" variant, and **every line naming every activity that asked for it**. Lines are grouped for the packer — 🛒 consumables / 🧺 equipment / 🖨️ print-at-desk — by transparent keyword rules printed in the core's header, print-at-desk winning first because the activity itself says the item is printed. The accounting is asserted, not hoped: raw materials in == occurrences out, and an activity that needs nothing is reported as packing nothing rather than silently vanishing. The composed kit renders as a **printable kit sheet** in the Day Planner's desk-paper style (ruled lines, red margin, the day it supports at the top, checkbox lines below), with a print stylesheet that prints the sheet alone, plus a copy-as-text twin in the established export pattern. Wired in like existing features: nav entry on its own page, a banner on `kit.html`, sitemap + llms.txt entries (llms links 37 → 38), the After-School Kit untouched beneath its banner. New suite `scripts/test-kitbag.js`: **1,028 assertions pass** (scenario/role shape, fitsScenario honesty incl. the 3-only rainy record refusing age 4 and an outdoor record refusing the indoor bag, per-age pool honesty across ages 3–10, the role-ladder fallback reaching the scenario pool and no further, compose determinism and no-repeats at five ages, honest-empty composition, swap cycling, the merge rules — shared glue sticks listed once with both activities named and both verbatim quantity phrasings preserved — classification rules, full aggregation provenance over a real composed bag incl. every real material string surviving verbatim, kit-list text, page/print wiring). The pre-packed age-6 bag demonstrates the merge live on the rendered page: "paper (one sheet per kid) — for: Paper Boat Race, Halloween Dots & Boxes · also listed as: paper".

**Part 1 — Twelve more activities banded for three-year-olds only (+12 → 649):** `big-gold-coin-pot-carry` (holidays 7 → 8 — St. Patrick's Day named as itself and the pot of gold kept a pretend story, said out loud; palm-sized coins carried in two hands and dropped in with a clink the whole group can hear grow fuller), `first-firefly-blink-count` (seasons 8 → 9 — a summer dusk count kept in chorus through long quiet watches; catch nothing, jar nothing, the group stays on the blanket), `big-pillow-mountain-climb` and `blanket-sled-slow-pull` (rainy-day 10 → 12 — a pillow mountain climbed hands-and-knees with the summit sit beheld by the whole line, jumping off never a descent; and a blanket sled pulled by the grown-up only, at walking pace, departing on the rider's own "Ready!"), `big-smell-jar-sniff-and-say` (stem 8 → 9 — foods and plant things only, essential oils banned outright, allergy list checked first, an honest "I don't know" a complete turn), `big-felt-quilt-wall-build` (team-community 7 → 8 — no grid, no outline; felt squares pressed edge to edge, crooked squares stay placed), `big-hat-pass-and-say` (icebreakers 8 → 9 — the song chooses the wearer; two sentences in a ridiculous hat, the hat washed like the travelling laundry it is), `big-texture-feet-path-walk` (outdoor 8 → 9 — four textures reported in foot words, the path walked barefoot by the adult first, every session), `big-pot-lid-cymbal-band` (music-rhythm 8 → 9 — the silence practised before any lids come out; stopping together is the hardest thing a band can do), `big-finger-paint-sky-sheet` (crafts 9 → 10 — fingers are the only brushes; sky first, clouds after, the white arriving as an event), `big-beanbag-balance-walk` (pe-movement 9 → 10 — "it fell, you fixed it, you are walking," resumed from that spot, never from the start), `big-peg-clip-card-row` (quiet-focus 12 → 13 — the pinch taught first, on the adult's own finger; a peg that pops off was hungry, not failed). **The headline narrowed for the fourth wave running:** age-3 coverage 94 → **106**, age 4 **unchanged at 160**, gap 66 → **54**; 3-only records 36 → 48; early-years-only 87 → 99; **Part 4 — FLAGSHIP: the Day Planner (`day.html`)** 🗓️ — a compose-your-day experience built on the planner core's conventions, in the teacher's-desk identity: five slots in a real day's order (🚌 Arrival 8:00, 🌅 Morning Meeting 9:00, 🧩 Centers 10:00, 🌳 Outdoor 11:30, 🌙 Closing 14:30), each filled from the real catalog through a new pure core (`assets/dayplanner-core.js`, UMD, no DOM): a slot's pool is filtered by the room's exact age against the real `age_min`/`age_max` fields, by the slot's minute budget against real `time_min`, by the slot's honest categories, and — for the outdoor slot — by a real outdoor location. Every slot offers its full honest pool in a picker, a 🎲 swap that cycles the pool deterministically and never duplicates another slot, and an **honest empty state**: a slot with no honest fit says so and stays open rather than stretching a record. The **🧸 Just Three starter day** is pre-composed and pre-loaded: arrival `name-echo-two-claps`, meeting `show-me-three-fingers`, centers `big-zip-up-down-board`, outdoor `grass-hill-roll-down`, closing `one-shake-pass-around` — every pick asserted banded 3-only and honestly fitting its slot, with an id-ordered 3-only fallback per slot and a null, never a borrowed older record, if a slot ever runs dry. The composed day renders as a **printable day sheet** — desk paper with ruled lines and a red margin, date, room age, per-slot clock, name, tagline, real facts, and a Bring list — with a print stylesheet that prints the sheet alone, plus a copy-as-text twin in the wave-7 export pattern. Wired in like existing features: nav entry on its own page, a banner on `planner.html`, the week planner untouched beneath it. ## Wave 31 — 2026-10-12 — "Little Hands, Just Three More" 🧦

**Part 1 — Twelve more activities banded for three-year-olds only (+12 → 625):** `big-heart-hand-delivery` (holidays 5 → 6 — one big heart carried in two hands to one named friend, and the adult's private delivery list guaranteeing every kid both gives one and gets one), `one-branch-bud-count` (seasons 6 → 7 — one low spring branch, buds counted by fingertip, one touch per number, the revisit promised on the record), `big-pot-stir-together` and `rain-drums-on-pots` (rainy-day 6 → 8 — pretend soup where nothing real goes in and nobody tastes it, dinosaur included by acclamation; and the rain played on upturned pots, fingertips for drizzle, palms for a five-second downpour), `big-funnel-sand-pour` (stem 6 → 7 — a wide funnel, a thin stream, a bottle that fills from the bottom up, and the tray that makes missing safe), `long-banner-carry-together` (team-community 5 → 6 — a hand every few steps along the edge or it folds; the halfway stop-and-hold is harder than the walking, and the crew can feel it), `how-i-got-here-moves` (icebreakers 6 → 7 — tell how you travel, the whole room copies your movement at your size; a telling is never corrected), `big-tree-hug-listen` (outdoor 6 → 7 — one adult-checked tree, palms flat or arms round, a real minute of listening, hugging offered never assigned), `sing-it-back-echo-lines` (music-rhythm 6 → 7 — one short line of a known song sung out and sung back whole; a small singer echoed by the entire room is a first solo), `paint-the-big-box-house` (crafts 7 → 8 — washable paint on a box big enough to stand inside, adults cut and never during the session, then the craft dries into the room's new building), `giant-steps-baby-steps` (pe-movement 7 → 8 — the caller names a step SIZE, everybody takes it together, and the no-out/no-sent-back promise is stated on the start line as the design, not a softening), `sock-pair-match-basket` (quiet-focus 10 → 11 — big clean odd socks matched by looking, the basket audited pair-by-pair as it is built, every finished pair rolled into a ball like real laundry). **The headline narrowed for the second wave running:** age-3 coverage 70 → **82**, age 4 **unchanged at 160**, gap 90 → **78**; 3-only records 12 → 24; early-years-only 63 → 75; **every primary category now carries at least 6 age-3 primaries** — and age 3 (82) passes ages 13/14 (76) to stop being the thinnest single age in the archive. ## Wave 30 — 2026-10-12 — "Little Hands, Just Three" 🎈

**Frontier chosen, and why:** wave 29's honest assessment named this wave outright — the age-3 headline (58 against 160 at age 4) "is structural… and closing it would take a different kind of wave (records banded 3-only), not another shelf of this kind." Four waves of broad early-years records (3–5 / 3–6) raised age 4 by exactly as much as age 3 every time. This is the different kind: **twelve records banded 3–3** — the first single-age bands in the archive. Why the band is honest and not a trick: these activities' whole design is a three-year-old moment (one shake of one tambourine passed hand to hand, a raindrop followed down a window with one finger, lids big enough for a whole palm), short (≤ 20 min), adult-led, big-piece, one-rule — and age 4, holding 160 records, does not miss them. Measured before this wave (primary category = `categories[0]`, records covering age 3): holidays / rainy-day / team-community were the floor at 4 each; seasons / stem / icebreakers / outdoor / music-rhythm 5 each; crafts 6, pe-movement 6, quiet-focus 9; rainy-day was also the thinnest primary category on the whole shelf (25 total).

**What shipped (three parts):**

**Part 1 — Twelve activities banded for three-year-olds only (+12 → 613):** one for every primary category, plus a second for rainy-day — `felt-tree-one-by-one` (holidays 4 → 5 — big felt pieces palm-pressed onto a low tree, counted as they go on, counted back down coming off), `sun-patch-shade-patch` (seasons 5 → 6 — stand in the sun patch, feel it, step into the shade, feel the change; the setup says plainly to wait for a day your own hands can feel the difference), `rain-call-to-the-rug` and `window-raindrop-follow` (rainy-day 4 → 6 — an adult-held umbrella as the rain signal, walked never run; and a real raindrop followed down real glass, an activity whose setup refuses to fake it), `big-magnet-wand-sticks` (stem 5 → 6 — encased wands only, big lids, "it sticks / it doesn't", the loose-magnet hazard stated in plain words on the record), `blanket-carry-teddy-to-bed` (team-community 4 → 5 — four corners, one passenger, Teddy is the only passenger, ever), `name-echo-two-claps` (icebreakers 5 → 6 — say your name, the whole circle says it back just like you said it, two claps; a whispered name gets the identical echo), `dandelion-clock-blow-walk` (outdoor 5 → 6 — the old counting game named as a tradition, pretend time said as pretend, untreated grass only, no exceptions), `one-shake-pass-around` (music-rhythm 5 → 6 — one tambourine, one shake, pass it on; the music is the waiting too), `sticky-window-big-shapes` (crafts 6 → 7 — it sticks and it un-sticks, so nothing can be put in the wrong place), `grass-hill-roll-down` (pe-movement 6 → 7 — the hug is the seatbelt, one roller at a time, the flag-caller's "Clear!" runs the hill), `big-lid-match-basket` (quiet-focus 9 → 10 — a wobble is information, and the decoy lid that genuinely fits nothing is a discovery, not an error). **The headline moved for the first time in five waves:** age-3 coverage 58 → **70**, age 4 **unchanged at 160**, gap 102 → **90**; early-years-only 51 → 63; **every primary category now carries at least 5 age-3 primaries.** Enrichment: copy_free 221 → 233 (the whole pack needs zero printing), no_elimination 70 → 75, inclusion 87 → 95. Tag deltas: Christmas 26 → 27, winter 114 → 115. Near-dupe screen in `scripts/add_wave30.py` — worst token Jaccard 0.24 (`name-echo-two-claps` vs `rhythm-echo-clap`, kept with the rationale in the script header: the older record builds rhythm patterns on names for 4–12s; this one is a five-minute first-days hello where copying the name exactly, kindly, is the whole game).

**Part 2 — Scout cycle: empty again, reported as empty (0 new candidates):** `pipeline/scan.py` ran live against all 6 feeds. The first attempt timed out on five feeds (one feed answered, 12 items); a confirming run minutes later reached all 6 (65 items: WeAreTeachers 16, Hands On As We Grow 7, Teach Beside Me 10, Little Bins 10, Kids Activities Blog 10, Buggy and Buddy 12) and still queued **nothing** — every relevant item was already in `pipeline/seen.json` (70 entries, unchanged) and the rest failed the KEEP/DROP filter. Queue unchanged and still fully decided: **60 entries, 22 accepted / 38 rejected / 0 pending**; `pipeline/review-queue.json` byte-identical before/after both runs and after the api.test.sh fixture pass (sha256 be7fbc15… on both sides throughout).

**Part 3 — New collection (20th): "Little Hands, Just Three"** 🎈 — the different shelf for the same room: the 12 new 3-only activities, each with a curator note in plain teacher voice ("It sticks and it un-sticks, so nothing can be put in the wrong place"). The four earlier Little Hands shelves stay intact at 12 picks each — they serve three-to-six; these twelve belong to the threes alone. `scripts/build.py`'s collection-count assertion moved 19 → 20 with it, as did `scripts/check_api.py` and the llms.txt bundle line.

**Completeness audit (run before finishing, as the standing rule requires):** every holiday tag in the vocabulary carries ≥ 5 activities (the floor is now July 4th / MLK Day / Indigenous Peoples' Day / Diwali / St. Patrick's Day at 5 each — no zero, no token shelf, and the winter holidays whose absence started the audit rule sit at Christmas 27 / winter season 115); season tags healthy (fall 51, spring 38, summer 29, winter 115); all 20 collections' picks resolve to real records, smallest collection is the pre-existing 10-pick "last-10-minutes"; thinnest primary categories are rainy-day 27 (up from 25), music-rhythm 31, seasons 33 — thin, not missing. Age remains the archive's known shape: 3 is still the thinnest single age at 70, now ahead of where 13/14 stood two waves ago and closing on their 76; subject keyword pass: literacy and math deep, drama (16) and coding (7) still thin by keyword and still — per the standing wave-26/27 call — not shelves the Vault promises, recorded not padded. **No conspicuously-missing item found.**
